%macro utl_etl_informacao(_informacao_,_etapa_) ;
	data _null_;
		put "NOTE: &_informacao_. da fase &_fase_.";
	run;

%mend utl_etl_informacao;

%Macro utl_etl_control_table(_tabela_);
proc sql;
	create table extracao.tb_controle_processo_&_data_referencia_. as
		select * from dictionary.tables
		where libname="EXTRACAO";
quit;

%Mend utl_etl_control_table;

%Macro utl_etl_extracao(_fase_,_base_,_distinct_,_chaves_,_variaveis_,_filtros_);

	%if %SYSFUNC(EXIST(EXTRACAO.tb_extracao_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.)) %then
		%do;

			data _null_;
				put "ERROR: TABELA tb_extracao_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_. JÁ EXISTE, VERIFIQUE NECESSIDADE DE SOBREPOSIÇÃO ";
			run;

		%end;
	%else
		%do;
			%utl_etl_informacao(_informacao_= Início da extração,_etapa_=&_fase_.)

			proc sql _method _tree;
			create table EXTRACAO.tb_extracao_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.(compress=CHAR) as
				select %if %length(&_distinct_.) ne 0 %then distinct;

				%do contador_chaves = 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_chaves_.));
					%scan(&_chaves_.,&contador_chaves.),
				%end;

			%do contador_vars = 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_variaveis_.));
				%if &contador_vars. < %sysfunc(countw(&_variaveis_.)) %then
					%scan(&_variaveis_.,&contador_vars.),;
				%else %scan(&_variaveis_.,&contador_vars.);
			%end;

			from &_base_. %if %length(&_filtros_.)>0  %then where   &_filtros_.
				%if %length(&_filtros_.)>0 %then

				%do;
					order by

						%do contador_chaves = 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_chaves_.));
							%if  &contador_chaves. < %sysfunc(countw(&_chaves_.)) %then
								%scan(&_chaves_.,&contador_chaves.),;
							%else %scan(&_chaves_.,&contador_chaves.);
						%end;
				%end;
			;
			;
			%do contador_chaves = 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_chaves_.));
				create index %scan(&_chaves_.,&contador_chaves.) on EXTRACAO.tb_extracao_RWA_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.(%scan(&_chaves_.,&contador_chaves.));
			%end;

			%if %sysfunc(countw(&_chaves_.)) > 1 %then
				%do;
					create index _index_key_ on EXTRACAO.tb_extracao_RWA_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.(
						%do contador_chaves = 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_chaves_.));
							%if &contador_chaves.< %sysfunc(countw(&_chaves_.)) %then
							%scan(&_chaves_.,&contador_chaves.),;
							%else %scan(&_chaves_.,&contador_chaves.) );
				%end;

		%end;;
	quit;

	%utl_etl_informacao(_informacao_= Término da extração,_etapa_=&_fase_.)
	%end;
%mend utl_etl_extracao;

%macro utl_etl_extracao_csv(_fase_,_filename_,_variaveis_,_length_,_format_,_informat_,_input_,_filtros_);

%put &_fase_.;
%put &_filename_.;
%put &_variaveis_.;
%put &_length_.;
%put &_format_.;
%put &_informat_.;
%put &_input_.;
%put &_filtros_.;
%put %length(&_filtros_.);

	%if %SYSFUNC(EXIST(EXTRACAO.tb_extracao_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.)) %then
		%do;

			data _null_;
				put "ERROR: TABELA tb_extracao_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_. JÁ EXISTE, VERIFIQUE NECESSIDADE DE SOBREPOSIÇÃO ";
			run;

		%end;
	%else
		%do;
			%utl_etl_informacao(_informacao_= Início da extração,_etapa_=&_fase_.)

		FILENAME dir_orig " &_filename_." ;
				data EXTRACAO.TB_EXTRACAO_RWA_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.%if %length(&_filtros_.) > 0  %then(where=(&_filtros_.));;
					LENGTH
						%do _contador_= 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_variaveis_.));
							%scan(&_variaveis_.,&_contador_.,"|") %scan(&_length_.,&_contador_.,"|")
						%end;
							;
					FORMAT
						%do _contador_= 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_variaveis_.));
							%scan(&_variaveis_.,&_contador_.,"|") %scan(&_format_.,&_contador_.,"|")
						%end;
						;
					INFORMAT
						%do _contador_= 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_variaveis_.));
							%scan(&_variaveis_.,&_contador_.,"|") %scan(&_informat_.,&_contador_.,"|")
						%end;
						;

	            INFILE dir_orig
                DELIMITER = ';'
                MISSOVER
                DSD
				FIRSTOBS=2;
				input
						%do _contador_= 1 %to %sysfunc(countw(&_variaveis_.));
							%scan(&_variaveis_.,&_contador_.,"|") %str(:) %scan(&_input_.,&_contador_.,"|")
						%end;
				;

				run;
			%utl_etl_informacao(_informacao_= Término da extração,_etapa_=&_fase_.)
		%end;
%mend utl_etl_extracao_csv;

%macro utl_etl_transformacao(_fase_,_query_);

	%if %SYSFUNC(EXIST(TRANSFOR.tb_transform_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.)) %then
		%do;

			data _null_;
				put "ERROR: TABELA TRANSFOR.tb_transform_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_. JÁ EXISTE, VERIFIQUE NECESSIDADE DE SOBREPOSIÇÃO ";
			run;

		%end;
	%else
		%do;
			%utl_etl_informacao(_informacao_= Início da transformação,_etapa_=&_fase_.)
			proc sql _method _tree;
			create table TRANSFOR.tb_transform_rwa_&_data_referencia_._&_fase_.(compress=CHAR) as
				&_query_.
				quit;

			%utl_etl_informacao(_informacao_= Término da transformação,_etapa_=&_fase_.)
		%end;
%mend utl_etl_transformacao;

%macro utl_etl_shiftrefm(_referencia_,_shift_);
	%sysfunc(intnx(month,%sysfunc(mdy(%substr(&_referencia_,5,2),1,%substr(&_referencia_,1,4))),&_shift_.),yymmn6.)
%mend utl_etl_shiftrefm;

%macro utl_etl_compressao(_etapa_);
	%if %length(&_etapa_.)>0 and %eval(&_etapa_. = 1) %then
		x "bzip2 /riscosbv/integrado/INFRA_1/RWACPAD/ETL/EXTRACAO/tb_extracao_rwa_%utl_etl_shiftrefm(&_data_referencia_.,-6)*.sas7bdat &";
	;
	x
	%if %length(&_etapa_.)>0 and %eval(&_etapa_. = 2) %then

		x "bzip2 /riscosbv/integrado/INFRA_1/RWACPAD/ETL/TRANSFORMACAO/tb_transform_rwa_%utl_etl_shiftrefm(&_data_referencia_.,-6)*.sas7bdat &";
	;
%mend utl_etl_compressao;

%macro utl_etl_descompressao(_etapa_);
	%if %length(&_etapa_.)>0 and %eval(&_etapa_. = 1) %then
		x "bzip2 -d /riscosbv/integrado/INFRA_1/RWACPAD/ETL/EXTRACAO/tb_extracao_rwa_&_data_referencia_.*.sas7bdat &";
	;
	%if %length(&_etapa_.)>0 and %eval(&_etapa_. = 2) %then
		x "bzip2 -d /riscosbv/integrado/INFRA_1/RWACPAD/ETL/TRANSFORMACAO/tb_transform_rwa_&_data_referencia_.*.sas7bdat &";
	;
%mend utl_etl_descompressao;

%macro utl_etl_agendador(_time_);

	data _null_;
		sleeptime="&sysdate.: &_time_."dt-datetime();
		call sleep(sleeptime,1);
	run;

%mend utl_etl_agendador;

%end;
%mend utl_etl_controller_utilidade;
%utl_etl_controller_utilidade

%put _all_;

FILENAME dir_orig "/riscosbv/integrado/INFRA_1/SICQ/MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.csv" ;
DATA repli.MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS;
    LENGTH
        count              8
        CdPessoa           8
        NmPessoa         $ 60 ;
    FORMAT
        count            BEST2.
        CdPessoa         BEST32.
        NmPessoa         $CHAR60. ;
    INFORMAT
        count            BEST2.
        CdPessoa         BEST32.
        NmPessoa         $CHAR60. ;
    INFILE dir_orig
		DELIMITER = ';'
		MISSOVER
		DSD
		FIRSTOBS=2;
    INPUT
        count            : ?? BEST2.
        CdPessoa         : ?? BEST32.
        NmPessoa         : $CHAR60. ;
RUN;


PROC SQL NOPRINT;
CREATE TABLE REPLI.DMT_SICQ_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_001 (compress=yes) AS
	SELECT
		A.NuContaContabil,
		A.NuObjetoNegociacao,
		A.CdGrupoConciliacao,
		A.pDATASOURCE,
		A.TIPO_EXPOSICAO,
		A.CdCategoriaContabil,
		A.CdEstrategiaOperacao,
		A.CdIndexador,
		A.CdOperacaoLegado,
		A.CdPessoaNegociador,
		A.CdFilial,
		A.NmFilial,
		A.CdParte,
		A.CdDivisaoCmrclClnte,
		A.CdDivisaoCmrclOfcr,
		A.QtPosicaoObjetoNegociacao,
		A.CdBook,
		A.CdVeiculoLegal,
		A.DtReferencia,
		A.CdClienteRisco,
		A.CdModalidadeCatalogoProduto,
		A.CdModalidadeCatalogoProdutoOrg,
		A.CdLinhaDRE,
		A.CdSituacaoPosicao,
		A.CdChaveNegocio,
		A.CdAtivo,
		A.CdRatingContabil,
		A.TPnegociacao,
		A.CdContraparte,
		A.CdProcesso,
		A.DtInicioOperacao,
		A.DtFimOperacao,
		A.NuCpfCnpj_parte,
		A.CdCatalogoProduto,
		A.DtVencimentoOperacao,
		A.VrCompromissada,
		A.CDGRUPO,
		A.FlEmpresaLigada,
		A.FlGarantia,
		A.FlHedgeAccounting,
		A.StLinha,
		A.TpContabil,
		A.TpNaturezaContaContabil,
		A.TpContabilizacao,
		A.TpOpcao,
		A.VrResultadoPDDContabil,
		A.VrSaldoMercado,
		A.VrSaldoPrincipalAtual,
		A.VrAjusteMTM,
		A.VrEntrada,
		A.VrGAPCompromissada,
		A.VrSaida,
		A.VrSaldoContaContabil,
		A.SgMoeda,
		A.DsMoeda,
		A.CdMoeda,
		A.NMGRUPO,
		A.NMGRUPOCONCILIACAO,
		A.DSMODALIDADECATALOGOPRODUTO,
		A.MNMODALIDADECATALOGOPRODUTO,
		A.NMCATALOGOPRODUTO,
		A.CDFAMILIA,
		A.NMFAMILIA,
		A.CP_VrFaturamentoAnual,
		A.CR_VrFaturamentoAnual,
		A.CR_CdNaturezaJuridica,
		A.CR_DsRamoAtividade,
		A.CR_CdUf,
		A.CR_CdPais,
		A.CP_CdRamoAtividade,
		A.CP_FlClienteEstrangeiro,
		A.CR_CdRamoAtividade,
		A.CR_FlClienteEstrangeiro,
		CASE
			WHEN A.CR_TpPessoa EQ '' THEN K.TpPessoa
			ELSE A.CR_TpPessoa
		END AS CR_TpPessoa,
		A.CR_CdTipoRelacionamento,
		A.CR_CPF_CNPJ,
		A.CP_CPF_CNPJ,
		A.CP_CNPJ_RAIZ,
		A.CP_CdNaturezaJuridica,
		A.CP_DsRamoAtividade,
		A.CR_CNPJ_RAIZ,
		CASE
			WHEN A.CP_TpPessoa EQ '' THEN K.TpPessoa
			ELSE A.CP_TpPessoa
		END AS CP_TpPessoa,
		A.CP_CdPais,
		A.CP_CdTipoRelacionamento,
		A.CP_CdUf,
		A.CP_NmSegmentoMercado,
		A.CR_NmSegmentoMercado,
		A.CdAtivoObjeto,
		A.FlEmissorEmpresaLigada,
		A.VrGAP,
		A.VrPDDGer,
		A.NuCpfCnpjContraparte,
		A.QtDiaAtraso,
		A.QtDiaMediaPonderada,
		A.CdClassificacaoRamoAtividade,
		A.CdClassificacaoSetorAtividade,
		A.CdIndexadorOriginal,
		A.CdOperacao,
		A.CdPraca,
		A.CdRatingAtraso,
		A.CdRatingCliente,
		A.CdRatingGerencial,
		A.CdRatingOperacao,
		A.CdTipoIndexador,
		A.CdTipoOperacao,
		
		A.FlInstituicaoFinanceira,
		A.NmEmissor,
		A.NuAgrupadorCOE,
		A.NuCnpjVeiculoLegal,
		A.NuContratoOriginal,
		A.NuCpfCnpjParte,
		A.PcIndexadorPos,
		A.PcIndexadorPosTIR,
		A.TpCliente,
		A.TpOrigemSistema,
		A.TpRenegociacao,
		A.VrPDDCet,
		A.VrPrincipalEmissao,
		A.VrPUPosicao,
		A.VrSaldoContabil,
	    B.CdGrupoEconomico AS CR_CdGrupoEconomico LENGTH=8 FORMAT=11. INFORMAT =11.,
	    B.NmGrupoEconomico AS CR_NmGrupoEconomico LENGTH=35 FORMAT=$35. INFORMAT =$35.,
	    C.CdGrupoEconomico AS CP_CdGrupoEconomico LENGTH=8 FORMAT=11. INFORMAT =11.,
	    C.NmGrupoEconomico AS CP_NmGrupoEconomico LENGTH=35 FORMAT=$35. INFORMAT =$35.
	FROM REPLI.DMT_SICQ_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO AS A
	LEFT JOIN
		_PEPRDMT.VWPESSOAGRUPOECONOMICO AS B
		ON (A.CdClienteRisco EQ B.CdPessoa)
	LEFT JOIN
		_PEPRDMT.VWPESSOAGRUPOECONOMICO AS C
		ON (A.CdContraParte EQ C.CdPessoa)
	LEFT JOIN
		(SELECT DISTINCT * FROM (
			SELECT
				D.CdPessoa,
				D.TpPessoa
			FROM _PEPRDMT.VWPESSOA AS D
			INNER JOIN
				(SELECT
					CdPessoa,
					CdTipoRelacionamento
				FROM _PEPRDMT.EXT_VWPESSOARELACIONAMENTO
					WHERE CdTipoRelacionamento EQ 13) AS E
				ON D.CdPessoa=E.CdPessoa
			UNION ALL
			SELECT
				F.CdPessoa,
				F.TpPessoa
			FROM _PEPRDMT.VWPESSOA AS F
				WHERE CdPessoa IN
					(SELECT DISTINCT CdContraparte FROM PEPREXT.EXT_FATO_RESULTADO_CTB
					UNION ALL
					SELECT DISTINCT CdClienteRisco FROM PEPREXT.EXT_FATO_RESULTADO_CTB))
		) AS G
			ON (A.CdClienteRisco EQ G.CdPessoa)
	LEFT JOIN
		(SELECT DISTINCT * FROM (
			SELECT
				H.CdPessoa,
				H.TpPessoa
			FROM _PEPRDMT.VWPESSOA AS H
			INNER JOIN
				(SELECT
					CdPessoa,
					CdTipoRelacionamento
				FROM _PEPRDMT.EXT_VWPESSOARELACIONAMENTO
					WHERE CdTipoRelacionamento EQ 13) AS I
				ON H.CdPessoa=I.CdPessoa
			UNION ALL
			SELECT
				J.CdPessoa,
				J.TpPessoa
			FROM _PEPRDMT.VWPESSOA AS J
				WHERE CdPessoa IN
					(SELECT DISTINCT CdContraparte FROM PEPREXT.EXT_FATO_RESULTADO_CTB
					UNION ALL
					SELECT DISTINCT CdClienteRisco FROM PEPREXT.EXT_FATO_RESULTADO_CTB))
		) AS K
			ON (A.CdContraParte EQ K.CdPessoa);
QUIT;

%macro rcSet(error);
   %if (&error gt &trans_rc) %then
      %let trans_rc = &error;
   %if (&error gt &job_rc) %then
      %let job_rc = &error;
%mend rcSet;

%macro rcSetDS(error);
   if &error gt input(symget('trans_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('trans_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
   if &error gt input(symget('job_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('job_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
%mend rcSetDS;

data LisPCOMtDir_Entrada;
   infile 
   		  '/riscosbv/integrado/INFRA_1/REPLISICQ/ListPCOMDir_Entrada.txt'
          lrecl = 256
          delimiter = ' '
          firstobs = 1;
   ;
   attrib Permission length = $15
      format = $15.
      informat = $15.;
   attrib NoFile length = 8
      format = 1.
      informat = 1.;
   attrib Size length = $30
      format = $30.
      informat = $30.;
   attrib Mes length = $3
      format = $3.
      informat = $3.;
   attrib Dia length = $2
      format = $2.
      informat = $2.;
   attrib HoraMinSeg length = $15
      format = $15.
      informat = $15.;
   attrib Ano length = $4
      format = 4.
      informat = 4.;
   attrib NmArquivo length = $100
      format = $100.
      informat = $100.;

   input Permission NoFile Size Mes Dia HoraMinSeg Ano NmArquivo;

   attrib TimeStampFile format=datetime23.3 informat=datetime23.3;
   TimeStampFile =input(cats(Dia,upcase(Mes),Ano,":",HoraMinSeg),datetime23.3);
run;

options symbolgen;
proc sql noprint;
select cats("'","&dir_entrada.",NmArquivo,"'") into: DirOuterNmFile
from LisPCOMtDir_Entrada
having max(TimeStampFile) EQ TimeStampFile;
quit;

%put &DirOuterNmFile.;

%rcSet(&sqlrc);

LIBNAME PEPREXT BASE '/riscosbv/integrado/INFRA_1/REPLISICQ/';
%rcSet(&syslibrc);

%let SYSLAST = %nrquote(work.W1D7XIEU);

%global etls_sql_pushDown;
%let etls_sql_pushDown = -1;
option DBIDIRECTEXEC;

%global etls_tableExist;
%global etls_numIndex;
%global etls_lastTable;
%let etls_tableExist = -1;
%let etls_numIndex = -1;
%let etls_lastTable = &SYSLAST;




%macro etls_loader;

   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   %let etls_tableExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM, VIEW)));

   %if &etls_tableExist %then
   %do;
      %let etls_hasPreExistingConstraint=0;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
      quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=PEPREXT;modify EXT_PCOM;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = PEPREXT nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete EXT_PCOM;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end; 


%if (&etls_tableExist eq 0) %then
%do;  

   %put %str(NOTE: Creating table ...);

   data PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM;
      attrib Scenario length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'Scenario';
      attrib Untitled1 length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'untitled1';
      attrib POS_Produto_Gerencial length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'POS_Produto_Gerencial';
      attrib Template length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'Template';
      attrib POS_ID length = $255
         format = $255.
         informat = $255.
         label = 'POS_ID';
      attrib POS_Veiculo length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'POS_Veiculo';
      attrib POS_Carteira_Gerencial length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'POS_Carteira_Gerencial';
      attrib POS_Book_Gerencial length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'POS_Book_Gerencial';
      attrib POS_Gap_Gerencial length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'POS_Gap_Gerencial';
      attrib POS_Bacen length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'POS_Bacen';
      attrib POS_OffShore length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'POS_OffShore';
      attrib POS_Grupo_Contabil_Bacen length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'POS_Grupo_Contabil_Bacen';
      attrib POS_Indexador_Gerencial length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'POS_Indexador_Gerencial';
      attrib POS_Tipo_Negociacao length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'POS_Tipo_Negociacao';
      attrib Currency length = $30
         format = $30.
         informat = $30.
         label = 'Currency';
      attrib Contract_Size_ARA length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'Contract_Size_ARA';
      attrib Cash_Flow length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'Cash_Flow';
      attrib POS_Position_Units length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'POS_Position_Units';
      attrib THEO_Value length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'THEO_Value';
      attrib THEO_Exposure length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'THEO_Exposure';
      attrib POS_Valor_Contabil_Gerencial length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'POS_Valor Contabil Gerencial';
      attrib POS_Valor_Presente_Gerencial length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'POS_Valor Presente Gerencial';
      attrib Pcom_Mercadoria length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'Pcom_Mercadoria';
      attrib Pcom_Sinal length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'Pcom_Sinal';
      attrib BR_Exposicao_Pcom length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'BR_Exposicao_Pcom';
      attrib BR_Exposicao_Pcom_EB length = $10
         format = $10.
         informat = $10.
         label = 'BR_Exposicao_Pcom_EB';
      attrib BR_Exposicao_Pcom_EL length = $40
         format = $40.
         informat = $40.
         label = 'BR_Exposicao_Pcom_EL';
      attrib untitled2 length = $1
         format = $1.
         informat = $1.
         label = 'untitled2';
      call missing(of _all_);
      stop;
   run;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

%end;  


%put %str(NOTE: Appending data ...);

proc append base = PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM
   data = &etls_lastTable (&etls_tableOptions)  force ;
 run;

%rcSet(&syserr);

%mend etls_loader;
%etls_loader;

%let etls_recCheckExist = 0;
%let etls_recnt = 0;
%macro etls_recordCheck;
   %let etls_recCheckExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM, VIEW)));

   %if (&etls_recCheckExist) %then
   %do;
      %local etls_syntaxcheck;
      %let etls_syntaxcheck = %sysfunc(getoption(syntaxcheck));
      
      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = PEPREXT.EXT_PCOM out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;
%etls_recordCheck;

%global etls_tableExist;

%global etls_lastTable;

%let etls_numIndex = -1;

%macro etls_loader;

   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   %let etls_tableExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PACS, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PACS, VIEW)));

   %if &etls_tableExist %then
   %do;
      %let etls_hasPreExistingConstraint=0;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
      quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=PEPREXT.EXT_PACS, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=PEPREXT;modify EXT_PACS;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = PEPREXT nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete EXT_PACS;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end; 


%if (&etls_tableExist eq 0) %then
%do;  

   %put %str(NOTE: Creating table ...);

   data PEPREXT.EXT_PACS;
      attrib Scenario length = $40;
      attrib Untitled2 length = $40;
      attrib POS_Produto_Gerencial length = $40;
      attrib Template length = $40;
      attrib POS_ID length = $255;
      attrib POS_Veiculo length = $30;
      attrib POS_Carteira_Gerencial length = $30;
      attrib POS_Book_Gerencial length = $30;
      attrib POS_Gap_Gerencial length = $30;
      attrib POS_Bacen length = $30;
      attrib POS_OffShore length = $30;
      attrib POS_Grupo_Contabil_Bacen length = $40;
      attrib POS_Indexador_Gerencial length = $40;
      attrib POS_Tipo_Negociacao length = $30;
      attrib Currency length = $30;
      attrib Contract_Size_ARA length = $40;
      attrib Cash_Flow length = $40;
      attrib POS_Position_Units length = $40;
      attrib THEO_Value length = $40;
      attrib THEO_Exposure length = $40;
      attrib POS_Valor_Contabil_Gerencial length = $40;
      attrib POS_Valor_Presente_Gerencial length = $40;
      attrib Pacs_Emissor length = $40;
      attrib Pacs_Pais length = $40;
      attrib Pacs_Indice length = $40;
      attrib Pacs_Sinal length = $10
         format = $10.
         informat = $10.;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELA_Exterior length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELA_Exterior_CD length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELA_Exterior_CND length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELA_Pais length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELA_Pais_CD length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELA_Pais_CND length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELI_Exterior length = $40;
      attrib Exposicao_Pacs_ELI_Pais length = $40;
      call missing(of _all_);
      stop;
   run;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

%end;  


%put %str(NOTE: Appending data ...);

proc append base = PEPREXT.EXT_PACS
   data = &etls_lastTable (&etls_tableOptions)  force ;
 run;

%rcSet(&syserr);

%mend etls_loader;
%etls_loader;

%let etls_recCheckExist = 0;
%let etls_recnt = 0;
%macro etls_recordCheck;
   %let etls_recCheckExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PACS, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(PEPREXT.EXT_PACS, VIEW)));

   %if (&etls_recCheckExist) %then
   %do;
      %local etls_syntaxcheck;
      %let etls_syntaxcheck = %sysfunc(getoption(syntaxcheck));
      
      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = PEPREXT.EXT_PACS out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=repli.DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=repli;modify DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = repli nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end;

%let trans_rc = 0;
%let etls_stepStartTime = %sysfunc(datetime(), datetime20.);

%global etls_sql_pushDown;
%let etls_sql_pushDown = -1;
option DBIDIRECTEXEC;

%global etls_tableExist;
%global etls_numIndex;
%global etls_lastTable;
%let etls_tableExist = -1;
%let etls_numIndex = -1;
%let etls_lastTable = &SYSLAST;




%macro etls_loader;

   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete WXZAWM9;
   quit;

   %put %str(NOTE: Mapping columns ...);

   proc sql;
      create table work.WXZAWM9 as
         select
            DsOrigemBackOffice,
            DtReferencia,
            TIPO_ACAO,
            pDATASOURCE,
            NuContaContabil,
            NuObjetoNegociacao length = 50
               format = $CHAR50.
               informat = $CHAR50.,
            CdGrupoConciliacao,
            CdCliente_ContraParte,
            CdCliente_ClienteRisco,
            CdModalidadeCatalogoProduto
      from &etls_lastTable
      ;
   quit;

   %let SYSLAST = work.WXZAWM9;

   %let etls_lastTable = &SYSLAST;
   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   %let etls_tableExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(repli.LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_UPDATE, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(repli.LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_UPDATE, VIEW)));

   %if &etls_tableExist %then
   %do;
      %let etls_hasPreExistingConstraint=0;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
      quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=repli.LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_UPDATE, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=repli;modify LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_UPDATE;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = repli nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_UPDATE;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end; 


%if (&etls_tableExist eq 0) %then
%do;  

   %put %str(NOTE: Creating table ...);

   data repli.LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_UPDATE;
      attrib DsOrigemBackOffice length = $50
         format = $CHAR50.
         informat = $CHAR50.;
      attrib DtReferencia length = 8
         format = YYMMN6.
         informat = YYMMN6.;
      attrib TIPO_ACAO length = $100
         format = $100.
         informat = $100.;
      attrib pDATASOURCE length = $20
         format = $CHAR20.
         informat = $CHAR20.;
      attrib NuContaContabil length = $50
         format = $CHAR50.
         informat = $CHAR50.;
      attrib NuObjetoNegociacao length = $50
         format = $CHAR50.
         informat = $CHAR50.;
      attrib CdGrupoConciliacao length = 8
         format = BEST6.
         informat = BEST6.;
      attrib CdCliente_ContraParte length = 8
         format = BEST6.
         informat = BEST6.;
      attrib CdCliente_ClienteRisco length = 8
         format = BEST6.
         informat = BEST6.;
      attrib CdModalidadeCatalogoProduto length = 8
         format = BEST6.
         informat = BEST6.;
      call missing(of _all_);
      stop;
   run;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

%end;  


%put %str(NOTE: Appending data ...);

proc append base = repli.LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_UPDATE
   data = &etls_lastTable (&etls_tableOptions)  force ;
 run;

%rcSet(&syserr);

proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
   delete WXZAWM9;
quit;

%mend etls_loader;
%etls_loader;

from WEGB9L2
where COMPRESS(TIPO_ACAO) EQ "INSERIR" and compress(NuContaContabil || NuObjetoNegociacao) not in
      (select distinct compress(A.NuContaContabil || A.NuObjetoNegociacao) from repli.LOG_ERROR_BACKOFFICE_INSERT AS A);

           " value(s) will be set to missing: CdCategoriaContabil,"
           " CdEstrategiaOperacao, CdIndexador, CdOperacaoLegado,   "
           " CdPessoaNegociador, CdParte, CdDivisaoCmrclClnte,"
           " CdDivisaoCmrclOfcr,    CdBook, CdLinhaDRE, CdSituacaoPosicao,"
           " CdChaveNegocio, CdAtivo,    CdRatingContabil,tpnegociacao, CdProcesso,"
           " FlEmpresaLigada, FlGarantia,    FlHedgeAccounting, StLinha,"
           " TpNaturezaContaContabil, TpContabilizacao,   "

           " SgMoeda,    DsMoeda, CdMoeda, CdPessoaEmpresaBv,"
           " CR_VrFaturamentoAnual,    CR_CdNaturezaJuridica,"
           " CR_DsRamoAtividade, CR_CdUf, CR_CdPais,    CR_CdRamoAtividade,"
           " CR_FlClienteEstrangeiro, CR_TpPessoa,    CR_CdTipoRelacionamento,"
           " CR_CPF_CNPJ, CP_CNPJ_RAIZ, CP_CdNaturezaJuridica,   "
           " CP_DsRamoAtividade, CR_CNPJ_RAIZ, CP_TpPessoa,"
           " CP_CdTipoRelacionamento,    CP_CdUf, CP_NmSegmentoMercado,"
           " CR_NmSegmentoMercado, CdAtivoObjeto,    FlEmissorEmpresaLigada,"
           " VrPDDGer, QtDiaMediaPonderada,    CdClassificacaoRamoAtividade,"
           " CdClassificacaoSetorAtividade,    CdIndexadorOriginal, CdOperacao,"
           " CdPraca, CdRatingAtraso, CdRatingCliente,    CdRatingGerencial,"
           " CdRatingOperacao, CdTipoIndexador, CdTipoOperacao,   "
           " DtRenegociacao, FlInstituicaoFinanceira, NmEmissor, NuAgrupadorCOE,"
           "    NuCnpjVeiculoLegal, NuContratoOriginal, NuCpfCnpjParte,"
           " PcIndexadorPos,    PcIndexadorPosTIR, TpCliente, TpOrigemSistema,"
           " TpRenegociacao, VrPDDCet,    VrPrincipalEmissao, VrPUPosicao,"
           " VrSaldoContabil";
   run;

   %put %str(NOTE: Mapping columns ...);
   proc sql;
      create view work.WXZAXC8 as
         select
            DsOrigemBackOffice,
            (substr(compress(NuContaContabil),1,25)) as NuContaContabil length = 25
               format = $25.
               informat = $25.,
            (substr(compress(NuObjetoNegociacao),1,20)) as NuObjetoNegociacao length = 20
               format = $20.
               informat = $20.,

               format = 11.
               informat = 11.,
            pDATASOURCE length = 30
               format = $30.
               informat = $30.,
            ('CONTRATO') as TIPO_EXPOSICAO length = 10
               format = $10.
               informat = $10.,
            . as CdCategoriaContabil length = 8
               format = 11.
               informat = 11.,
            . as CdEstrategiaOperacao length = 8
               format = 11.
               informat = 11.,
            . as CdIndexador length = 8
               format = 11.
               informat = 11.,
            "" as CdOperacaoLegado length = 20
               format = $20.
               informat = $20.,
            . as CdPessoaNegociador length = 8
               format = 11.
               informat = 11.,
            . as CdParte length = 8
               format = 11.
               informat = 11.,

               format = 11.
               informat = 11.,
            . as CdDivisaoCmrclOfcr length = 8

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   delete W1H2LK40;
quit;

%put %str(NOTE: Mapping columns ...);
proc sql;
   create table work.W1H2LK40 as
      select distinct
         NuObjetoNegociacao,
         CdContraparte,
         CdClienteRisco,
         CdVeiculoLegal
   from &SYSLAST
      where substr(compress(nucontacontabil),1,2) in ('16', '18','49')
         and cdgrupoconciliacao NE 14
   order by
      CdContraparte desc
   ;
quit;

%let SYSLAST = work.W1H2LK40;

%let etls_sql_pushDown = &sys_sql_ip_all;

      data = &etls_lastTable (&etls_tableOptions)  force ;

      delete WXZAXFJ;

proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
   delete WYFOXK;
quit;

proc sql;
   create table work.WYFOXK as
   select distinct
      case when compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil  ) =compress( WWKY8T.NuContaContabil)  and
                           compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao  ) = compress(WWKY8T.NuObjetoNegociacao ) and
                          DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.CdGrupoConciliacao    = WWKY8T.CdGrupoConciliacao
                then WWKY8T.VrPDDAtual
                 else DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.VrPDDAtual
      end as VrPDDAtual length = 8
         format = COMMAX17.2
         informat = COMMAX17.2,
      case when compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil  ) =compress( WWKY8T.NuContaContabil)  and
                           compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao  ) = compress(WWKY8T.NuObjetoNegociacao ) and
                          DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.CdGrupoConciliacao   = WWKY8T.CdGrupoConciliacao
                then WWKY8T.VrPDDCompl
                 else DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.VrPDDCompl
      end as VrPDDCompl length = 8
         format = COMMAX17.2
         informat = COMMAX17.2,
      case when compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil  ) =compress( WWKY8T.NuContaContabil)  and
                           compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao  ) = compress(WWKY8T.NuObjetoNegociacao ) and
                           DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.CdGrupoConciliacao   = WWKY8T.CdGrupoConciliacao
                then WWKY8T.TIPO_ACAO
                 else DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.TIPO_ACAO
      end as TIPO_ACAO length = 100
         format = $100.
         informat = $100.,
      DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao length = 20
         format = $CHAR20.
         informat = $CHAR20.
         label = 'NuObjetoNegociacao',
      DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil length = 50
         format = $CHAR50.
         informat = $CHAR50.
         label = 'NuContaContabil',
      DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.CdGrupoConciliacao length = 8
         format = 11.
         informat = 11.
         label = 'CdGrupoConciliacao',
      DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.CdVeiculoLegal length = 8
         format = 11.
         informat = 11.
         label = 'CdVeiculoLegal',
      CASE WHEN SUBSTR(compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao),1,3) NE '611' AND
				  SUBSTR(compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil ),1,3) NOT IN ('189' '169') THEN
			      CASE WHEN compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil ) =compress( WWKY8T.NuContaContabil)  and
			                compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao ) = compress(WWKY8T.NuObjetoNegociacao) and
			                DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.CdGrupoConciliacao  = WWKY8T.CdGrupoConciliacao THEN WWKY8T.VrSaldoContaContabil
			      	   ELSE DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.VrSaldoContaContabil
			      END
			 ELSE DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.VrSaldoContaContabil
		END AS VrSaldoContaContabil length = 8
         format = 20.6
         informat = 20.6
         label = 'VrSaldoContaContabil',
		
		CASE WHEN SUBSTR(compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao),1,3) NE '611' AND
				  SUBSTR(compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil ),1,3) NOT IN ('189' '169') THEN
	      		CASE WHEN compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuContaContabil  ) =compress( WWKY8T.NuContaContabil)  and
	                	compress(DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.NuObjetoNegociacao  ) = compress(WWKY8T.NuObjetoNegociacao ) and
	                	DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.CdGrupoConciliacao   = WWKY8T.CdGrupoConciliacao THEN WWKY8T.DsOrigemBackOffice
	            	 ELSE  DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.DsOrigemBackOffice
	      		END
			ELSE DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.DsOrigemBackOffice
		END AS DsOrigemBackOffice length = 50
         format = $CHAR50.
         informat = $CHAR50.,
      DMT_INT_SICQ_EXPO_ATDO_SICQINSR.pDATASOURCE length = 30
         format = $30.

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';

            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

%macro rcSet(error);
   %if (&error gt &trans_rc) %then
      %let trans_rc = &error;
   %if (&error gt &job_rc) %then
      %let job_rc = &error;
%mend rcSet;

%macro rcSetDS(error);
   if &error gt input(symget('trans_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('trans_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
   if &error gt input(symget('job_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('job_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
%mend rcSetDS;

%macro etls_loader;

   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   %let etls_tableExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_IFBCMOSEG, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_IFBCMOSEG, VIEW)));

   %if &etls_tableExist %then
   %do;
      %let etls_hasPreExistingConstraint=0;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
      quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_IFBCMOSEG, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=PEPRDMT;modify DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_IFBCMOSEG;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = PEPRDMT nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_IFBCMOSEG;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end; 


%if (&etls_tableExist eq 0) %then

%macro rcSet(error);
   %if (&error gt &trans_rc) %then
      %let trans_rc = &error;
   %if (&error gt &job_rc) %then
      %let job_rc = &error;
%mend rcSet;

%macro rcSetDS(error);
   if &error gt input(symget('trans_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('trans_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
   if &error gt input(symget('job_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('job_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
%mend rcSetDS;

%macro etls_loader;

   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   %let etls_tableExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_PRJFIN, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_PRJFIN, VIEW)));

   %if &etls_tableExist %then
   %do;
      %let etls_hasPreExistingConstraint=0;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
      quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_PRJFIN, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=REPLI;modify DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_PRJFIN;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = PEPRDMT nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_PRJFIN;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end; 


%if (&etls_tableExist eq 0) %then
%do;  

   %put %str(NOTE: Creating table ...);

%macro etls_startPerformanceStats;
   %log4sas();
   %log4sas_logger(Perf.ARM, 'level=info');
   options armagent=log4sas armsubsys=(ARM_PROC);
   %global _armexec;
   %let _armexec = 1;
   %perfinit(applname="&applName");
   %global etls_recnt;
   %let etls_recnt=-1;
%mend;
%etls_startPerformanceStats;

%macro etls_setArmagent;
   %let armagentLength = %length(%sysfunc(getoption(armagent)));
   %if (&armagentLength eq 0) %then
      %do;
         %log4sas();
         %log4sas_logger(Perf.ARM, 'level=info');
         options armagent=log4sas armsubsys=(ARM_PROC);
      %end;
%mend etls_setArmagent;

%macro etls_setPerfInit;
   %if "&_perfinit" eq "0" %then
      %do;
         %etls_setArmagent;
         %global _armexec;
         %let _armexec = 1;
         %perfinit(applname="&applName");
      %end;
%mend etls_setPerfInit;


%global job_rc trans_rc sqlrc;
%let sysrc = 0;
%let job_rc = 0;
%let trans_rc = 0;
%let sqlrc = 0;
%global etls_stepStartTime;

data _null_; run;

%macro rcSet(error);
   %if (&error gt &trans_rc) %then
      %let trans_rc = &error;
   %if (&error gt &job_rc) %then
      %let job_rc = &error;
%mend rcSet;

%macro rcSetDS(error);
   if &error gt input(symget('trans_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('trans_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
   if &error gt input(symget('job_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('job_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
%mend rcSetDS;

		  when WXC23W8.CdContraparte  = MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa
        	  and MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa  <> WXC23W8.CdVeiculolegal

         on
         (
            WXC23W8.CdContraparte = MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa
            and MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa <> WXC23W8.CdVeiculolegal
            and WXC23W8.CdContraparte NOT IS MISSING
         )
   ;
quit;

%let etls_recCheckExist = 0;
%let etls_recnt = 0;
%macro etls_recordCheck;
   %let etls_recCheckExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(work.WXH2NZ8, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(work.WXH2NZ8, VIEW)));

   %if (&etls_recCheckExist) %then
   %do;
      %local etls_syntaxcheck;
      %let etls_syntaxcheck = %sysfunc(getoption(syntaxcheck));
      
      options nosyntaxcheck;

      proc contents data = work.WXH2NZ8 out = work.etls_contents(keep = nobs) noprint;
      run;

      data _null_;
         set work.etls_contents (obs = 1);
         call symput("etls_recnt", left(put(nobs,32.)));
      run;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_contents;
      quit;

      
      options &etls_syntaxcheck;
   %end;
%mend etls_recordCheck;
%etls_recordCheck;

      case when WXH2NZ8.CdClienteRisco   = MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa
                       and WXH2NZ8.CdVeiculolegal <> MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa
                       and WXH2NZ8.CdClienteRisco NOT IS MISSING  then '2'
                else WXH2NZ8.FlEmpresaLigada

      work.WXH2NZ8 as WXH2NZ8 left join
      PEPRDMT.MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS as MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS
         on
         (
            WXH2NZ8.CdClienteRisco = MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa
            and WXH2NZ8.CdVeiculolegal <> MAP_EMPRESAS_LIGADAS.CdPessoa
            and WXH2NZ8.CdClienteRisco NOT IS MISSING
         )
   ;
quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_EMPRLG, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=REPLI;modify DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_EMPRLG;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = PEPRDMT nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete DMT_AUX_EXPOSICAO_ATACADO_EMPRLG;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end;

%macro rcSet(error);
   %if (&error gt &trans_rc) %then
      %let trans_rc = &error;
   %if (&error gt &job_rc) %then
      %let job_rc = &error;
%mend rcSet;

%macro rcSetDS(error);
   if &error gt input(symget('trans_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('trans_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
   if &error gt input(symget('job_rc'),12.) then
      call symput('job_rc',trim(left(put(&error,12.))));
%mend rcSetDS;

%macro etls_loader;

   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   %let etls_tableExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_INDEXREGUL, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_INDEXREGUL, VIEW)));

   %if &etls_tableExist %then
   %do;
      %let etls_hasPreExistingConstraint=0;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
      quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_INDEXREGUL, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=REPLI;modify DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_INDEXREGUL;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = REPLI nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_INDEXREGUL;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end; 


%if (&etls_tableExist eq 0) %then

%mend rcSetDS;

%global etls_tableExist;

%macro etls_loader;

   %let etls_tableOptions = ;

   
   %let etls_tableExist = %eval(%sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_PACSPCOM, DATA)) or
         %sysfunc(exist(REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_PACSPCOM, VIEW)));

   %if &etls_tableExist %then
   %do;
      %let etls_hasPreExistingConstraint=0;

      proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
         delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
      quit;

      %let etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable=0;

      %macro etls_CIContents(table=,workTableOut=,inDSOptions=);
         %put NOTE: Building table listing Constraints and Indexes for: &table;
         proc datasets lib=work nolist; delete &workTableOut; quit;
         proc contents data=&table&inDSOptions out2=&workTableOut noprint; run;

         data &workTableOut;
            length name $60 type $20 icown idxUnique idxNoMiss $3 recreate $600;
            name = '';
            type = '';
            icown = '';
            idxUnique = '';
            idxNoMiss = '';
            recreate = '';
            set &workTableOut;
            ref = '';
            type=upcase(type);
            if type eq 'REFERENTIAL' then
            do;
               put "WARNING%QUOTE(:) Target table is referenced by constraints in"
                    " another table: " ref;
               call symput('etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable','1');
               delete;
            end;
            if type='INDEX' and ICOwn eq 'YES' then delete;
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

      %mend etls_CIContents;

      %etls_CIContents(table=REPLI.DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_PACSPCOM, workTableOut=etls_commands, inDSOptions=);

      %if &etls_otherTablesReferToThisTable %then
         %put WARNING%QUOTE(:) Replacing entire table will fail. Consider an alternate load technique or revising constraints.;
      %else
      %do; 

         data etls_commands_F;
            set etls_commands;
            if upcase(type)="FOREIGN KEY" then
            do;
               command='ic delete '||trim(name)||';';
               output etls_commands_F;
            end;
         run;

         %put %str(NOTE: Removing foreign keys before dropping table...);
         data _null_;
            set etls_commands_F end=eof;
            if _n_=1 then
               call execute('proc datasets nolist lib=REPLI;modify DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_PACSPCOM;');
            call execute(command);
            if eof then call execute('; quit;');
         run;
         %rcSet(&syserr);

   %end; 

   proc datasets lib = work nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete etls_commands etls_commands_F;
   quit;

   
   %put %str(NOTE: Dropping table ...);
   proc datasets lib = REPLI nolist nowarn memtype = (data view);
      delete DMT_AUX_EXPO_ATCDO_PACSPCOM;
   quit;

   %rcSet(&syserr);

   %let etls_tableExist = 0;

%end;

			LAST_TABLE.DtRenegociacao,
			LAST_TABLE.FlHedgeAccounting,
			LAST_TABLE.FlInstituicaoFinanceira,
			LAST_TABLE.NuCnpjVeiculoLegal,

			LAST_TABLE.VrPrincipalEmissao,
